A prior EIC disallowance (Form 8862) and Form 2555 (Step 1 question 3) are refused. Nonresident aliens (Step 1 question 4) are screened by scope.other_income. Choosing not to claim the credit and having the IRS figure it are not modeled.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (`../triage/eic.json`; 1,374 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 124 partial records; 1,998 comparisons; every completed-return difference inspected with the 6-witness cap lifted, T-083): 1,906 of 1,998 agree; 3 value, 38 candidate-unknown (8 on completed returns, 18 incomplete, 12 refused), 38 candidate-refuses, 9 project-unknown, 4 project-refuses. The completed candidate-unknowns are its Step 1 limit chosen by the count of qualifying children (eic.h_limit / eic.h_agi_ok), which needs each dependent's dependent.us_half even when line 11b is at or above the largest limit and question 1 is No for any count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. All 8 completed ones checked: stage8.hoh.child.ctc, stage8.single.dependent.boundaries, stage8.single.dependent.students_and_age17, stage8.single.dependent.household_and_ssn, audit.dependent.listed_not_dependent_no_tin and audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property have line 11b $120,000.00, stage8.single.phaseout.partial and audit.household.single_community_property $215,300.00, against a largest limit of $61,555.00. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need is a model error (README), as in Stage 19 round 2. The 3 value differences (generated 246, 288 and 323; project $0.00, candidate $205.00 / $538.00 / $94.00) are refused returns of married filing separately filers without a qualifying child and household.married false: Its Step 3/4 married test (eic.h_married_sep) requires household.married to be true even for married filing separately, so a separate filer recorded as unmarried passes Step 4, question 1; a married filing separately status is married by definition, and the project tests the status itself (eic.married_rules). i1040gi p. 41, Step 4, question 1: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household? No. Continue Yes. STOP You can’t take the credit." The 38 candidate-refuses are its declared gates (dependent.other_claimant, dependent.married, sa.excluded_income) on returns the project also refuses: scope. The 9 project-unknown withhold f1040.tax_exempt_interest (7 incomplete; Step 2 needs it, representation) or are partial records withholding eic.qc_of_another / eic.us_home on refused classm.asset.4. The 4 project-refuses are cited screens (scope.other_line16, sa.dependent, f8889 and retirement screens). Project stands.

Second transcription's note: The box is checked only at the STOPs whose text says to check it: Step 1 question 2, Step 3 question 6, Step 4 questions 3 and 5, Worksheet A line 2 zero, and Worksheet B line 4b zero or less, line 5 No, and line 7 zero. Each applies only if no earlier STOP was reached. The other STOPs give no credit without the box. Nonresident aliens are screened. Choosing not to claim the credit is not modeled.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c: "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a." i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (`../triage/eic.json`; 1,374 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 124 partial records; 1,998 comparisons; every completed-return difference inspected with the 6-witness cap lifted, T-083): 1,914 of 1,998 agree; 1 value, 46 candidate-unknown (13 on completed returns), 26 candidate-refuses, 11 project-refuses. The 13 completed candidate-unknowns are the eic.credit child-count limit unknowns (dependent.us_half), all on returns above the largest Step 1 limit (line 11b $120,000.00 or $215,300.00; stage13.ctc_spouse_ssn, stage13.odc_spouse_no_tin, stage13.ctc_spouse_no_id married filing jointly above $68,675.00): model error. The one value (generated 480, refused; project no, candidate yes) is a married filing separately filer with household.married false whom the candidate carries past Step 4, question 1 to a Worksheet A line 2 of zero: Its Step 3/4 married test (eic.h_married_sep) requires household.married to be true even for married filing separately, so a separate filer recorded as unmarried passes Step 4, question 1; a married filing separately status is married by definition, and the project tests the status itself (eic.married_rules). Refusals are cited gates on refused returns (scope). Project stands.

Continue No. Skip questions 4 and 5; go to question 6"; question 6: "No. Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"; amb.2025.separated_spouses_box (chosen reading: the box only on that Step 3 path). The 4 values (classm.eic.8 and .deepseek, generated 115 and 415; project yes, candidate no) are married filing separately filers with household.married false and eic.same_home false: Its Step 3/4 married test (eic.h_married_sep) requires household.married to be true even for married filing separately, so a separate filer recorded as unmarried passes Step 4, question 1; a married filing separately status is married by definition, and the project tests the status itself (eic.married_rules). The completed unknown (audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property, line 11b $120,000.00) is the child-count limit unknown of eic.credit. Refusals are cited gates on refused returns (scope). Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6: "This is your earned income credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (`../triage/eic.json`; 1,374 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 124 partial records; 1,998 comparisons; every completed-return difference inspected with the 6-witness cap lifted, T-083): 1,182 of 1,998 agree; 756 candidate-unknown (365 completed: 352 "no band" lookups above the table after Step 1 STOPs, 13 child-count limit unknowns), 29 candidate-shows-skipped (10 completed), 31 candidate-refuses. The candidate figures and shows line 6 on every return. Witnesses: stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn (completed; Step 1, question 2 STOP) project hidden, candidate $649.00; stage9.eic.no_child.over65 (Step 4, question 2 STOP) $308.00; stage16.cleanup.mut.clwb_three_figured (Step 2 STOP) $1,294.00. Refusals are cited gates on refused returns (scope). Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | household.credit_disallowed, s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| eic.check_27c | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 1) | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 2) | s1.line8_other, scope.other_income | s1.line8_other |
| (Step 5, 3) | s1.line8_other, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 4b | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 1 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 2 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 4 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 5 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.line6) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  # ---------------- Step 5 earned income worksheet ----------------
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero (an amount the tool does not support, or a credit that cannot be taken)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "Step 5 question 1 and the Clergy and Church employees rules replace line 1z with a reduced amount for clergy and church employees. Those situations are outside the tool and are covered by scope.schedule1, so the tool computes only the plain line 1z case and refuses otherwise."

  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s1.line8_other, scope.other_income]
    note: "Medicaid waiver payments and the Schedule 1 line 8s exclusion are outside the tool. The tool refuses when they are present, so this line is zero."

  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}

  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "Nontaxable combat pay is screened out by s8812.combat_pay, so the elected amount is zero in the case the tool computes."

  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
    note: "Within the tool, self-employment means a Schedule C. Other self-employment, clergy and church employee income are screened by person.se_screen and scope.schedule1, and statutory employees by sc.statutory. So the answer is yes exactly when there is a Schedule C (s1.has_sc)."

  - id: eic.h_se_screen_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons answering yes to the self-employment screening question (which includes ministers and church employee income)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.se_screen, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 1: Are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income of $108.28 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [scope.schedule1, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [eic.h_se_screen_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "The interface has no direct clergy or church employee question. The chosen reading is yes only when both screens that mention clergy and church employee income are answered yes. In that case eic.step5_line1 refuses, so a computed return always has this as false (and Form 1040 line 27b unchecked)."

  # ---------------- Worksheet B, Parts 1 to 4 ----------------
  - id: eic.h_wsb_1e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 1e: Schedule SE, Part I, line 3 plus lines 4b and 5a, minus line 13 (you and your spouse, for those filing Schedule SE)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.eic_part1
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}
    note: "Reads the per-person catalog field person.eic_part1 as each person's Worksheet B Part 1 amount (line 1c minus line 1d). This meaning is inferred from the field name and could not be verified from the pages."

  - id: eic.h_wsb_2c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 2c: net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31 (self-employed not required to file Schedule SE)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.sc_total
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: false}]}
    note: "Reads person.sc_total as the person's total of Schedule C line 31. Line 2a (farm) is zero because farm income is screened by person.se_screen. Schedule K-1 amounts are screened by scope.schedule1."

  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_wsb_1e, eic.h_wsb_2c, eic.earned_income]}
    note: "Line 3 (statutory employee Schedule C line 1) is zero because statutory employees are screened by sc.statutory. Line 4a is the Step 5 earned income."

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "This line is reached only when line 4b is more than zero (otherwise STOP), so flooring at zero does not change any reached value."

  # ---------------- Children counts, joint, limits ----------------
  - id: eic.h_kids
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children: $19,104"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children, married filing jointly: $26,214"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child: $50,434"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child, married filing jointly: $57,554"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children: $57,310"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children, married filing jointly: $64,430"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children: $61,555"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children, married filing jointly: $68,675"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit for the number of children who have valid SSNs and filing status (Step 1 question 1, Step 5 question 3, Worksheet B line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_joint

  # ---------------- Step 1 ----------------
  - id: eic.h_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  # ---------------- Step 2 ----------------
  - id: eic.h_inv_7a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 7a (if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: eic.h_invest
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income = line 2a + line 2b + line 3b + line 7a"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_inv_7a]}

  - id: eic.h_invest_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: investment income is not more than $11,950"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_invest, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
    note: "If investment income is more than the limit, question 3 asks about Form 4797. Form 4797 is outside the tool (sd.other_forms), so the answer is No and the credit cannot be taken. Question 4 situations (Schedule E, Form 8814, rental of personal property, passive activities) are screened by scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16 and scope.other_income."

  - id: eic.h_through2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passed Steps 1 and 2"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_invest_ok, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- Step 3 ----------------
  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the purpose of claiming the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}

  - id: eic.h_s3_q5_fail
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 5 STOP: same principal residence for the last 6 months of 2025 and not legally separated and living apart"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_s3_q6_fail
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 6 STOP: you could be a qualifying child of another person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_fail, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_step3_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 completed with a qualifying child; go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_fail, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses (Step 3, question 6, No)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_through2, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q5_fail, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Step 3 question 6 says to check the box on any No answer. The Special rule for separated spouses applies only to married filers not filing jointly, so the box is checked only for filers who went through questions 3 to 5 (married, filing separately or head of household)."

  # ---------------- Step 4 ----------------
  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons (you, and your spouse if filing jointly) born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: Were you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "The Pub. 596 rule for a spouse or taxpayer who died in 2025 is not in the supplied text and is not encoded. The birth-date test is used as printed."

  - id: eic.h_s4_q3_fail
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 3 STOP: main home not in the United States for more than half of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_s4_q5_fail
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 5 STOP: you could be a qualifying child of another person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_step4_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 completed without a qualifying child; go to Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Question 6 (can you be claimed as a dependent) is read from sa.dependent. For the non-joint filers who reach question 6 there is no spouse, and a dual-status alien is outside the credit anyway."

  - id: eic.h_reached5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Passed Steps 1 to 4 and reached Step 5"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_through2, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_ok, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- Worksheet A (B Part 5) ----------------
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5 (Worksheet B line 6: your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.earned_income
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_6
    note: "Worksheet A lines 1 to 6 and Worksheet B lines 6 to 11 are the same computation. This line takes Worksheet B line 6 when the filer answered Yes to Step 5 question 2."

  - id: eic.h_tab0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no qualifying children, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tab1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one qualifying child, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tab2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two qualifying children, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tab3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more qualifying children, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_below_start
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount on line 1 is less than the first line of the EIC Table ($1)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_start, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_start, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_tab0
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_start, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_tab1
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_start, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_tab2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_start, {const: false}]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_tab3
    note: "The table starts at $1. An amount below $1 has no row, and this transcription reads it as a credit of zero. If line 2 is zero: STOP, the credit cannot be taken (credit zero, box 27c checked)."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_thr0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no qualifying children: $10,620"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.other}
  - id: eic.h_thr0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, no qualifying children, married filing jointly: $17,730"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_thr1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more qualifying children: $23,350"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.other}
  - id: eic.h_thr1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold, 1 or more qualifying children, married filing jointly: $30,470"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}

  - id: eic.h_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 threshold for the number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr0_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr0_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr1_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_joint, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_thr1_joint

  - id: eic.h_tab5_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no qualifying children, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tab5_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one qualifying child, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tab5_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two qualifying children, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_tab5_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more qualifying children, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.h_line5_figured
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_tab5_0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_tab5_1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_tab5_2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_tab5_3

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If the amount on line 3 is less than the threshold, leave line 5 blank; otherwise look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Enter the credit here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_figured
    note: "The IRS says to skip line 5 or leave it blank in the first two cases. A blank line is represented as zero, and line 6 does not read line 5 in those cases."

  - id: eic.h_line6_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_threshold]}]}
          then: eic.h_line6_min

  # ---------------- Eligibility, result and line 27c ----------------
  - id: eic.h_earned_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5 question 3 (Worksheet A filers) or Worksheet B lines 4b and 5: earned income more than zero (Worksheet B) and less than the limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No STOP was reached in Steps 1 to 5 or Worksheet B Part 4"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit (enter this amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line6
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed, sa.excluded_income]
    note: "Every STOP in Steps 1 to 5 and Worksheet B Part 4 makes the credit zero. A zero on line 2 (Worksheet B line 7) gives zero through line 6. A prior EIC disallowance (Form 8862) and Form 2555 (Step 1 question 3) are refused. Nonresident aliens (Step 1 question 4) are screened by scope.other_income. Choosing not to claim the credit and having the IRS figure it are not modeled."

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c (you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_through2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s3_q6_fail, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_through2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_q3_fail, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_through2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_s4_q5_fail, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_limit]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, le: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_reached5, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, eic.h_limit]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The box is checked only at the STOPs whose text says to check it: Step 1 question 2, Step 3 question 6, Step 4 questions 3 and 5, Worksheet A line 2 zero, and Worksheet B line 4b zero or less, line 5 No, and line 7 zero. Each applies only if no earlier STOP was reached. The other STOPs give no credit without the box. Nonresident aliens are screened. Choosing not to claim the credit is not modeled."
```
```
